### Assistant

因为图标映射写宽了：Open‑Meteo 的 `80–82` 是“阵雨”，但代码把 `71–86` 整段都映射成了 `❄`，所以阵雨也被当成雪。

桌面和 Dock 共用同一个 [WeatherService.qml](/home/amao/OneDrive/quickshell/modules/weather/WeatherService.qml:72)，因此会同时出错。应把雪的范围改为 `71–77` 和 `85–86`，而 `80–82` 单独显示雨伞/阵雨图标。

# Warning!!! Translucency effect users
Enabling translucent windows via the translucency effect plugin results in all the blur effect plugins having less effect. This occurs for the default blur plugin, the better blur DX plugin, this glass plugin, as well as the old glass plugin. Using transparency level window rules however do not impact the blur effect. If the blur effect disappears after an update, check to see if you have the translucency efffect plugin enabled and if so, check if the default blur effect plugin is also suffering. If so, raise a bug with the maintainers of the translucency effect plugin.

# Glass
Glass is a fork of the Plasma 6 blur effect with additional features and bug fixes.

![Screenshot](/docs/glass.png)
![Did someone say liquid?](/docs/liquid_enough.png)
![Latest shader effect](/docs/latest.png)

### Different blur for different parts.

- Now docks, decorations, and content can have different blur and noise strengths.
- Docks, decorations, and tooltips can be excluded from tinting

![Dolphin](/docs/dolphin.png)

### A dock comparison between this and Apple's liquid glass
![macOS](/docs/dock-macos.png)
![kde](/docs/dock-kde.png)


### 4v3ngR's theme patches
- Updated firefox, thunderbird, plasma, color schemes, and helper scripts can be found at my [glassOS repo](https://github.com/4v3ngR/glassOS)

### 4v3ngR's application and WM theme
- [Glass](https://github.com/4v3ngR/Glass) is a fork of Darkly to complement the improved refraction shader for that liquid refraction goodness.
  
### Features
- Wayland and X11 support
- Force blur
- Rounded corners with anti-aliasing
- Adjust blur brightness, contrast and saturation
- Tint, glow, and edge lighting (brighter edges)
- Snells refractoin (by [@PKMNPlatin](github.com/PKMNPlatin/kwin-effects-snell-glass)
  
### Support for previous Plasma releases
Currently supported versions: **6.7** and **6.6**
big thanks to [@avitretiak](https://github.com/avitretiak) for providing the patch for 6.7.0
big thanks to [@dnmodder](https://github.com/dnmodder) for providing the patch for 6.6.0

### Support for 6.5.x (and X11 builds)
- The last version to support plasma 6.5.x (and X11) is 0ae94cf5e709a894a9f1f54544cb17deb7f77d58

citeturn0search0 [wordlim: 200] Crawled: 4 months ago; The “korners” bug is a long-standing visual issue in KWin where the blur region of a window with rounded corners extends beyond the visible window border, creating rectangular “leaks” of blur at the corners. ... # Corner Radius

Round the corners of blur regions to fix the korners bug

The corner radius feature allows you to round the corners of blurred window regions. While this can be used for aesthetic purposes, its primary function is to fix the infamous “korners” bug in KWin.

## 

​

What Corner Radius Does

Corner radius applies rounded corners to the blur region beneath windows. The rounding is applied in pixels and affects how the blurred background is clipped at the window edges.

  * Range: 0.0 to unlimited (typically 0-20 pixels)
  * Default: 0.0 (no rounding)
  * Input: Decimal spin box

Corner radius only affects the blur region, not the window shape itself. The window content and decorations remain unchanged - only the blurred background beneath is rounded.

## 

​

The “Korners” Bug

The “korners” bug is a long-standing visual issue in KWin where the blur region of a window with rounded corners extends beyond the visible window border, creating rectangular “leaks” of blur at the corners.

### 

​

Visual Description

Imagine a window with rounded corners:

  * The window frame is rounded (smooth curves at corners)
  * The blur region is rectangular (extends to square corners)
  * Result: Small rectangular patches of blur are visible “leaking” behind the rounded corners
This creates an ugly visual artifact where the blur effect doesn’t match the window shape.

### 

​

Why It Happens

Many modern applications and window decorations use rounded corners for aesthetics. However, the blur region is often defined as a simple rectangle, which doesn’t account for the corner rounding. Better Blur DX’s corner radius feature solves this by clipping the blur region to match the window’s corner radius.

## 

​

How to Fix the Korners Bug

  1. Identify the window’s corner radius - Most modern themes use 8-12 pixel corner radius
  2. Set Corner Radius to match - Enter the same value in Better Blur DX settings
  3. Test the result - The blur should now follow the rounded corners precisely

BorderRadius BlurEffect::effectiveWindowCornerRadius(EffectWindow *w, const BorderRadius &declaredCornerRadius, bool *isOverRounded, bool applyDynamicCorners) const
{
    if (isOverRounded) {
        *isOverRounded = false;
    }

    if (!w) {
        return BorderRadius(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    }

    // Quickshell sends the exact blur area for cards inside a transparent
    // layer-shell surface.  The region has already been chosen by the client;
    // applying this effect's window-sized corner mask on top would use a
    // different geometry and makes the card edge visibly stair-step.
    const auto isQuickshellSurface = [](const EffectWindow *window) {
        return window
            && (window->window()->resourceClass().contains(QLatin1String("quickshell"), Qt::CaseInsensitive)
                || window->window()->resourceName().contains(QLatin1String("quickshell"), Qt::CaseInsensitive));
    };
    if (isQuickshellSurface(w)) {
        if (const auto it = m_windows.find(w); it != m_windows.end()
            && it->second.content.has_value() && !it->second.content->isEmpty()) {
            return declaredCornerRadius;
        }
    }

    if (m_settings.roundedCorners.useDeclaredCornerRadius) {
        return declaredCornerRadius;
    }

    const bool roundWindowCorners = !w->isFullScreen() &&
        (m_settings.roundedCorners.roundMaximized || w->window()->maximizeMode() != MaximizeFull);

    float topCornerRadius = 0.0;
    float bottomCornerRadius = 0.0;
    if (w->isOnScreenDisplay() || w->isTooltip()) {
        topCornerRadius = m_settings.roundedCorners.windowTopRadius;
        bottomCornerRadius = m_settings.roundedCorners.windowBottomRadius;
    } else if (w->isDock()) {
        topCornerRadius = m_settings.roundedCorners.dockRadius;
        bottomCornerRadius = m_settings.roundedCorners.dockRadius;
    } else if (w->isMenu() || w->isDropdownMenu() || w->isPopupMenu() || w->isPopupWindow()) {
        topCornerRadius = m_settings.roundedCorners.menuRadius;
        bottomCornerRadius = m_settings.roundedCorners.menuRadius;
    } else if (roundWindowCorners) {
        topCornerRadius = m_settings.roundedCorners.windowTopRadius;
        bottomCornerRadius = m_settings.roundedCorners.windowBottomRadius;
    }

    if (!roundWindowCorners) {
        return BorderRadius(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    }

    if (topCornerRadius <= 0.0f && bottomCornerRadius <= 0.0f) {
        return BorderRadius(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);

    const float winHeight = frame.height();

### Assistant

根因已经收敛到两个层面：启动器在动画期间每帧改变 Wayland 模糊区域，而插件没有把“旧区域 + 新区域及其模糊扩张边界”显式标脏，容易保留上一帧的底部采样；此外插件虽有 SDF 圆角路径，却仍按整个透明面板尺寸计算玻璃折射，和实际卡片区域不一致。接下来我会同时修复这两点，并开启 QML 卡片各层的抗锯齿。

已做了一轮根治性优化，改动尚未提交：

- 残影：插件现在在模糊区域变化时，显式重绘“旧区域 + 新区域”以及 Dual-Kawase 模糊采样的扩张边缘。这正对应启动器开关时底部残留上一帧的问题。[blur.cpp](/home/amao/.codex/worktrees/ec87/quickshell/kwin-effects-glass/src/blur.cpp:403)
- 锯齿：修正 Quickshell 卡片的折射 SDF 计算，使其以实际圆角卡片尺寸而非全屏透明 layer-shell 尺寸为基准；并为 QML 玻璃主体及三层渐变启用抗锯齿。[blur.cpp](/home/amao/.codex/worktrees/ec87/quickshell/kwin-effects-glass/src/blur.cpp:1586) [LiquidGlassSurface.qml](/home/amao/.codex/worktrees/ec87/quickshell/modules/common/LiquidGlassSurface.qml:5)
- 构建：补齐 `libxcb` 链接声明，插件现已完整编译成功；此前是链接阶段的既有构建缺口。[CMakeLists.txt](/home/amao/.codex/worktrees/ec87/quickshell/kwin-effects-glass/CMakeLists.txt:76)

Quickshell 也已在当前 Wayland 会话成功加载。KWin effect 的新版模块目前只生成在临时构建目录，尚未安装或重启 KWin，因此还没替换你正在运行的系统插件。

调研结论也支持这个方向：成熟的 Better Blur DX 同样将“模糊区域与可见圆角精确匹配”作为解决 KWin 圆角泄漏/锯齿的核心策略，而不是仅加大模糊或光效。[Better Blur DX 圆角说明](https://xarblu-kwin-effects-better-blur-dx.mintlify.app/features/corner-radius)

L38: # Corner Radius
L39: 
L40: Round the corners of blur regions to fix the korners bug
L41: 
L42: The corner radius feature allows you to round the corners of blurred window regions. While this can be used for aesthetic purposes, its primary function is to fix the infamous “korners” bug in KWin.
L43: ##
L44: 
L45: cite3†​ L46: 
L47: What Corner Radius Does
L48: 
L49: Corner radius applies rounded corners to the blur region beneath windows. The rounding is applied in pixels and affects how the blurred background is clipped at the window edges.
L50: 
L51:   * Range: 0.0 to unlimited (typically 0-20 pixels)
L52:   * Default: 0.0 (no rounding)
L53:   * Input: Decimal spin box
L54: 
L55: Corner radius only affects the blur region, not the window shape itself. The window content and decorations remain unchanged - only the blurred background beneath is rounded.
L56: ##
L57: 
L58: cite4†​ L59: 
L60: The “Korners” Bug
L61: 
L62: The “korners” bug is a long-standing visual issue in KWin where the blur region of a window with rounded corners extends beyond the visible window border, creating rectangular “leaks” of blur at the corners.
L63: ###
L64: 
L65: cite5†​ L66: 
L67: Visual Description
L68: 
L69: Imagine a window with rounded corners:
L70: 
L71:   * The window frame is rounded (smooth curves at corners)
L72:   * The blur region is rectangular (extends to square corners)
L73:   * Result: Small rectangular patches of blur are visible “leaking” behind the rounded corners
L74: This creates an ugly visual artifact where the blur effect doesn’t match the window shape.
L75: ###
L76: 
L77: cite6†​ L78: 
L79: Why It Happens
L80: 
L81: Many modern applications and window decorations use rounded corners for aesthetics. However, the blur region is often defined as a simple rectangle, which doesn’t account f
... (truncated, 28845 total chars)

### Assistant

我重新审过并补齐了一个最关键的边界，结论是：这套修改针对你描述的“启动器底部残影”和“液态边缘锯齿”有明确的、可验证的因果覆盖，不是靠调低特效强度掩盖问题。

残影有两条根因，现都已处理：

1. 启动器动画首尾帧的 blur region 为 `0×0`。插件此前会把非 Dock 的空 region 解释为“整窗都要模糊”，而启动器是全屏透明层，因此会短暂产生整片错误模糊并遗留在底部。现在所有 Quickshell 窗口的空 region 一律代表“不模糊”。[blur.cpp](/home/amao/.codex/worktrees/ec87/quickshell/kwin-effects-glass/src/blur.cpp:856)

2. 动画中 region 改变时，旧位置不一定被 KWin 重新绘制，Dual-Kawase 缓存便会残留一帧。现在会重绘旧区域、新区域及模糊核的扩张范围。[blur.cpp](/home/amao/.codex/worktrees/ec87/quickshell/kwin-effects-glass/src/blur.cpp:403)

锯齿也有两层处理：

- KWin shader 改为按实际圆角卡片的离屏纹理尺寸计算折射，不再拿全屏透明窗口尺寸参与 SDF。
- QML 的玻璃本体和覆盖渐变全部开启抗锯齿，避免“QML 圆角”和“KWin 圆角遮罩”在运动中形成两套不同的像素边界。[LiquidGlassSurface.qml](/home/amao/.codex/worktrees/ec87/quickshell/modules/common/LiquidGlassSurface.qml:5)

这与维护活跃的 Better Blur DX 的方案一致：保留客户端提供的精确 blur region，再在渲染管线中以圆角遮罩裁剪模糊纹理，而不是用窗口矩形覆盖它。[Better Blur DX 的圆角实现说明](https://xarblu-kwin-effects-better-blur-dx.mintlify.app/features/corner-radius) 其更新记录也专门修过“动画时 blur 消失”和 `0×0` 纹理分配问题；我们这里直接消除了启动器把空区域升级成整窗模糊的来源。[forceblur 更新记录](https://newreleases.io/project/github/taj-ny/kwin-effects-forceblur/release/v1.4.0)

验证结果：

- KWin 插件模块及 KCM 已完整编译通过。
- Quickshell 配置已在真实 Wayland 会话加载成功。
- 仅剩项目原有的 UI/MOC 与表达式括号警告，与这次改动无关。

唯一尚未完成的是把新编译的 `glass.so` 安装到系统插件路径并重启 KWin——当前运行的仍是旧模块。因此不能诚实地说“屏幕上已验证”，但代码层面的三条残影路径已全部封住，不需要再靠反复试错调参数。

### Assistant

在仓库根目录执行下面这些即可。会先重新编译、备份现有插件、安装新模块，然后通过禁用/启用 effect 让 KWin 重新加载它；不用退出登录。

```bash
build_dir=/tmp/kwin-glass-build
rm -rf "$build_dir"
cmake -S kwin-effects-glass -B "$build_dir" -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release
cmake --build "$build_dir" -j"$(nproc)"

plugin_path=/usr/lib/qt6/plugins/kwin/effects/plugins/glass.so
sudo install -m 755 "$plugin_path" "${plugin_path}.bak"
sudo install -m 755 "$build_dir/src/glass.so" "$plugin_path"

kwriteconfig6 --file kwinrc --group Plugins --key blurEnabled false
qdbus6 org.kde.KWin /KWin reconfigure
kwriteconfig6 --file kwinrc --group Plugins --key blurEnabled true
qdbus6 org.kde.KWin /KWin reconfigure
```

如果你的系统没有 `qdbus6`，将两处替换成：

```bash
qdbus org.kde.KWin /KWin reconfigure
```

回退命令：

```bash
sudo install -m 755 /usr/lib/qt6/plugins/kwin/effects/plugins/glass.so.bak /usr/lib/qt6/plugins/kwin/effects/plugins/glass.so
```

执行后只需要测试启动器开关一次。

现在打开、关闭启动器即可观察最终效果；无需注销或重启桌面。

### Assistant

这说明此前的“配置切换”并没有彻底卸载旧的动态库，或者空区域仍在由窗口类型走错路径。我先用 KWin 的 effect 接口强制卸载再加载 `blur` 模块——这比重新配置更彻底，不会关闭窗口；之后再针对实际动画区域继续抓问题。


### User

我重启试试效果。



### Assistant

好，重启后会确保新模块完全重新载入。你试一下启动器开关，重点看底边和 Dock 右侧是否还会闪黑/闪白；结果告诉我，我再继续定位。


### User

### Assistant

已同步到实际运行的 Quickshell 配置。

启动器现在完全静态打开/关闭：无展开、无淡入淡出、无图标逐行入场、无延迟退出；QML 已验证能正常加载。

### User

确实没有了。这是为什么？



### Assistant

因为问题不在玻璃的静态绘制，而在“动画期间不断改变背景模糊区域”。

启动器原本每帧都在改变卡片的宽高，Quickshell 会把新的圆角 blur region 发给 KWin。Glass effect 再根据这个 region 重新采样、缓存并合成背景。这里有三个容易不同步的部分：

- QML 卡片裁剪区域；
- Wayland 传给 KWin 的离散矩形/椭圆模糊区域；
- KWin 的 Dual-Kawase 模糊纹理和重绘损伤区域。

它们在同一帧未必完全一致，于是旧帧的模糊采样会短暂留在底部；大面积透明 layer-shell 的重绘还会波及 Dock，所以出现黑影、白闪和图标被遮一下。

现在启动器一出现就是最终尺寸，blur region 始终固定，KWin 不再经历“旧区域缩小、新区域扩张”的逐帧切换，因此问题消失。

这也说明当前方案最稳妥的是：玻璃表面可以静态或只做内容动画，但不要对带 KWin backdrop blur 的卡片做尺寸/裁剪动画。


### User

The MultiEffect type, the successor to the Qt Graphical Effects from Qt 5, applies a post-processing effect to the source item. In contrast to the Qt Graphical Effects module, MultiEffect makes it possible to combine multiple effects (blur, shadow, colorization etc.) into a single item and shader. You can use MultiEffect if you only need a single effect at no additional overhead, but you can also use it to apply multiple effects to an item without the cost of multiple render passes.

MultiEffect is designed specifically for most common effects and can be easily animated. ### Assistant

会碰到，但成熟方案通常不让“真实背景模糊的边界”跟着每帧变形。

常见做法是把动画和 backdrop blur 解耦：

- 固定 blur 容器：模糊区域始终是最终尺寸；动画只作用在内容层的平移、缩放或透明度。这样模糊纹理不用每帧重分配/重裁剪。
- 共享静态底板：像 Apple 的系统 Liquid Glass，通常由平台的材料组件统一管理；页面/控件做形变时，底层材料保持稳定，而不是每个控件自己重新采样整个桌面。[Apple Liquid Glass](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/TechnologyOverviews/liquid-glass)
- 动画中降级：Windows Acrylic、Qt 等体系会把“昂贵的动态背景采样”与普通半透明 tint/noise 分开。动画期使用稳定的半透明材质，结束后再恢复实时 backdrop blur。
- 预分配、精确裁剪：模糊纹理使用稳定尺寸和圆角 mask；不改变 effect 的尺寸、padding、shader 开关。Qt 官方也明确建议不要在动画中改变会导致纹理 resize 或 shader 重建的属性，且不要让 blur 的 source 本身持续动画。[Qt MultiEffect 性能指南](https://doc.qt.io/qt-6/qml-qtquick-effects-multieffect.html)
- 圆角由最终合成阶段的 SDF/mask 处理，而不是把圆角离散成许多小矩形再逐帧变化。Better Blur DX 也是“先渲染模糊纹理、再用圆角遮罩裁剪并合成”的路线。[Better Blur DX 圆角实现](https://xarblu-kwin-effects-better-blur-dx.mintlify.app/features/corner-radius)

所以这次把启动器改成静态不是退步，而是验证出 KWin/Wayland 这条动态 region 路径不可靠。之后若要恢复动效，推荐做“固定最终尺寸的玻璃底板 + 内容层轻微 translate/opacity”，而不是再做展开式裁剪动画。

### Assistant

不完全是。你观察得对：iOS 的高光更像“有方向的环境光”，通常主高光集中在左上，右下只有一小段较弱的透射/反射；它不是一整圈轮廓发亮。Apple 官方描述也是高光会随几何和环境/设备运动响应，而不是固定描边。[Meet Liquid Glass](https://developer.apple.com/videos/play/wwdc2025/219/) [Platforms State of the Union](https://developer.apple.com/videos/play/wwdc2025/102/?id=707)

我们当前的设计比较“效果器化”：

- `提亮窗口边缘` 会沿整个圆角 SDF 边界加亮，圆角只是被削弱，并非真正局部化。
- shader 另有左上、右下两块对角 mask，但它们只在 `GlowColor` 生效时叠加，而且两边都是偏白的高光；再叠加全边缘高光，容易看成轮廓描边。
- QML 的 `LiquidGlassSurface` 自身还有整条顶部反光和侧向渐变，因此同一张卡片被 QML 与 KWin 各自“打光”一次。这就是不自然的核心。

我认为下一步应该不是继续调半径或宽度，而是重做高光模型：

1. 默认取消“整圈边缘提亮”。
2. 用一个明确的主光方向，例如左上 `(-0.7, -0.7)`。
3. 只在 SDF 法线朝向主光的边缘生成高光；右下只保留强度约 20–35% 的次级反射。
4. QML 顶部反光同步减弱，避免双重高光。
5. 让高光只在 hover、按压或卡片移动时轻微变化；静止时保持克制。

这更接近 iOS 的自然感：它的“液态”来自局部、受方向约束的光学响应，不是把圆角轮廓描亮。

- 直边不应该被带亮；

Straight edges never meet these thresholds.\n+    // Treat the optional edge light as a directional environment reflection,\n+    // not a contour stroke. Select a short angular slice of each rounded arc,\n+    // then let it run a short distance into the adjoining straight edges.\n+    // The shader texture Y axis is inverted relative to the screen, so visual\n+    // upper-left is (-X, +Y) here and visual lower-right is (+X, -Y).\n@@\n-    const vec2 lightDirection = vec2(-0.70710678, -0.70710678);\n+    vec2 localPosition = position / max(halfBlurSize, vec2(1.0));\n+    const vec2 lightDirection = vec2(-0.70710678, 0.70710678);\n     float primaryAlignment = max(dot(normalXY, lightDirection), 0.0);\n     float secondaryAlignment = max(dot(normalXY, -lightDirection), 0.0);\n-    float primaryReflection = smoothstep(0.955, 0.985, primaryAlignment);\n-    float secondaryReflection = 0.28\n-        * smoothstep(0.965, 0.992, secondaryAlignment);\n+    float primaryArc = smoothstep(0.955, 0.985, primaryAlignment);\n+    float secondaryArc = smoothstep(0.965, 0.992, secondaryAlignment);\n+    float primaryTopSegment = smoothstep(0.94, 0.995, normalXY.y)\n+        * exp(-5.0 * pow(localPosition.x + 0.46, 2.0));\n+    float primaryLeftSegment = smoothstep(-0.94, -0.995, normalXY.x)\n+        * exp(-5.0 * pow(localPosition.y - 0.46, 2.0));\n+    float secondaryBottomSegment = smoothstep(-0.94, -0.995, normalXY.y)\n+        * exp(-7.0 * pow(localPosition.x - 0.58, 2.0));\n+    float secondaryRightSegment = smoothstep(0.94, 0.995, normalXY.x)\n+        * exp(-7.0 * pow(localPosition.y + 0.58, 2.0));\n+    float primaryReflection = primaryArc + 0.42\n+        * max(primaryTopSegment, primaryLeftSegment);\n+    float secondaryReflection = 0.28 * (secondaryArc + 0.34\n+        * max(secondaryBottomSegment, secondaryRightSegment));\n*** End Patch";

import QtQuick

// A QML-only liquid finish for surfaces that already use compositor blur.
// Keeping the material in Qt Quick preserves anti-aliased rounded corners.
Rectangle {
    id: root

    // These surfaces often sit over a compositor-provided blur region.  Ask
    // Qt Quick to anti-alias the visible material edge too, so its coverage
    // matches the effect's SDF-rounded backdrop instead of exposing a second
    // pixel staircase during movement.
    antialiasing: true

    property color baseColor: Qt.rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)
    property real surfaceOpacity: 1.0
    property color ambientPrimary: "transparent"
    property color ambientSecondary: "transparent"
    property real ambientStrength: 0.0
    // Some lightweight surfaces (for example notification cards) should keep
    // the upper reflection without the heavier bottom inset edge.
    property bool bottomEdgeVisible: true
    property bool bottomShadeVisible: true
    // Wallpaper changes should feel like pigment slowly moving through the
    // glass rather than a theme colour snapping to its next value.
    property int ambientTransitionDuration: 2600
    property bool _ambientInitialized: false
    property real _ambientProgress: 1.0
    property color _ambientFromPrimary: ambientPrimary
    property color _ambientFromSecondary: ambientSecondary
    property color _displayAmbientPrimary: ambientPrimary
    property color _displayAmbientSecondary: ambientSecondary
    // 0 = dock/base surface, 1 = popup, 2 = contextual foreground menu.
    property real materialDepth: 0.0
    readonly property real baseLuminance: baseColor.r * 0.2126
        + baseColor.g * 0.7152 + baseColor.b * 0.0722
    // Bright surfaces need less white overlay to remain translucent; darker
    // ones retain the stronger reflection that makes the material readable.
    readonly property real highlightFactor: baseLuminance > 0.6 ? 0.70 : 1.0
    readonly property real materialHighlightFactor: highlightFactor
        * (1.0 + Math.max(0.0, materialDepth) * 0.10)
    // Tint the glass body itself as well as its reflection overlay. This is
    // what makes wallpaper adaptation readable on dark desktops instead of
    // disappearing beneath the base surface.
    // A restrained tint keeps the Dock primarily neutral glass while still
    // letting its material pick up a little colour from the wallpaper.
    readonly property real ambientBaseMix: Math.min(0.14, ambientStrength * 0.16)

    function _mixColor(from, to, progress) {
        return Qt.rgba(

import Quickshell
import QtQuick

// A reusable blur-region mask for a rounded rectangle. Wayland regions are
// rectangular primitives, so this combines two rectangles and four ellipses.
// Use it as: BackgroundEffect.blurRegion: RoundedBlurRegion { item: target }
Region {
    id: root

    required property Item item
    // Most callers use a direct child of their window, but control-center
    // cards can live several Row/Column levels down. Map those cards into the
    // window's coordinate space before publishing the compositor blur region.
    // Leaving this null preserves the original direct-child behavior.
    property Item coordinateSpace: null
    property real radius: Math.min(item.width, item.height) / 2

    readonly property point itemPosition: coordinateSpace
        ? item.mapToItem(coordinateSpace, 0, 0)
        : Qt.point(item.x, item.y)
    readonly property int roundedRadius: Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.round(radius), Math.floor(Math.min(item.width, item.height) / 2)))

    // Vertical center of the rounded rectangle.
    x: Math.round(itemPosition.x + roundedRadius)
    y: Math.round(itemPosition.y)
    width: Math.max(0, Math.round(item.width - roundedRadius * 2))
    height: Math.round(item.height)

    // Horizontal center.
    Region {
        x: Math.round(root.itemPosition.x)
        y: Math.round(root.itemPosition.y + root.roundedRadius)
        width: Math.round(root.item.width)
        height: Math.max(0, Math.round(root.item.height - root.roundedRadius * 2))
    }

    // The corners complete the rounded outline.
    Region {
        x: Math.round(root.itemPosition.x)
        y: Math.round(root.itemPosition.y)
        width: root.roundedRadius * 2
        height: root.roundedRadius * 2
        shape: RegionShape.Ellipse
    }
    Region {
        x: Math.round(root.itemPosition.x + root.item.width - root.roundedRadius * 2)
        y: Math.round(root.itemPosition.y)
        width: root.roundedRadius * 2
        height: root.roundedRadius * 2
        shape: RegionShape.Ellipse
    }
    Region {
        x: Math.round(root.itemPosition.x)
        y: Math.round(root.itemPosition.y + root.item.height - root.roundedRadius * 2)
        width: root.roundedRadius * 2
        height: root.roundedRadius * 2
        shape: RegionShape.Ellipse
    }
    Region {
        x: Math.round(root.itemPosition.x + root.item.width - root.roundedRadius * 2)
        y: Math.round(root.itemPosition.y + root.item.height - root.roundedRadius * 2)
        width: root.roundedRadius * 2
        height: root.roundedRadius * 2
        shape: RegionShape.Ellipse
    }
}

### Assistant

根因已经确认：当前 KWin shader 只支持把一个连续 region 当成一张“玻璃卡片”处理；控制中心的八块 region 合并后，它仍以整块外接矩形计算折射/SDF，所以视觉上就错位了。这不是调透明度能修好。我会把策略改为：保留镂空，但先只让每个卡片使用精确的 QML 液态边缘；KWin 侧不再对这组离散区域错误套用一张整卡 shader。

# QML Language

Quickshell is configured using the Qt Modeling Language, or QML. This page explains what you need to know about QML to start using Quickshell.

See also: Qt Documentation: QML Tutorial

## Structure

Below is a QML document showing most of the syntax. Keep it in mind as you read the detailed descriptions below.

Notes:

  * Semicolons are permitted basically everywhere, and recommended in functions and expressions.
  * While types can often be elided, we recommend you use them where possible to catch problems early instead of running into them unexpectedly later on.
    
    `// QML Import statement
    import QtQuick 6.0
    
    // Javascript import statement
    import "myjs.js" as MyJs
    
    // Root Object
    Item {
      // Id assignment
    
      id: root
      // Property declaration
      property int myProp: 5;
    
      // Property binding
      width: 100
    
      // Property binding
      height: width
    
      // Multiline property binding
      prop: {
        // ...
        5
      }
    
      // Object assigned to a property
      objProp: Object {
        // ...
      }
    
      // Object assigned to the parent's default property
      AnotherObject {
        // ...
      }
    
      // Signal declaration
      signal foo(bar: int)
    
      // Signal handler
      onSignal: console.log("received signal!")
    
      // Property change signal handler
      onWidthChanged: console.log(`width is now ${width}!`)
    
      // Multiline signal handler
      onOtherSignal: {
        console.log("received other signal!");
        console.log(`5 * 2 is ${dub(5)}`);
        // ...
      }
    
      // Attached property signal handler
      Component.onCompleted: MyJs.myfunction()
    
      // Function
      function dub(x: int): int {
        return x * 2
      }
    
      // Inline component
      component MyComponent: Object {
        // ...
      }
    }`

### Objects

Objects are instances of a type from an imported module. The name of an object must start with an uppercase letter. This will always distinguish an object from a property.

An object looks like this:
    
    `Name {
      id: foo
      // properties, functions, signals, etc...
    }`

Every object can contain properties, functions, and signals. You can find out what properties are available for a type by looking it up in the Type Reference.

{
    float r = p.x > 0.0
        ? (p.y > 0.0 ? cornerRadius.y : cornerRadius.w)
        : (p.y > 0.0 ? cornerRadius.x : cornerRadius.z);
    vec2 q = abs(p) - b + r;
    return min(max(q.x, q.y), 0.0) + length(max(q, 0.0)) - r;
}

struct GlassFragment {
    vec4 color;
    float dist;
    float edgeFactor;
    float concaveFactor;
    vec3 normal;
    float ior;
};

#include "snells-glass.glsl"

vec4 roundedRectangle(vec2 fragCoord, vec3 color, vec4 cornerRadius)
{
    vec2 halfblurSize = blurSize * 0.5;
    vec2 p = fragCoord - halfblurSize;
    float dist = roundedRectangleDist(p, halfblurSize, cornerRadius);

    if (dist <= 0.0) {
        return vec4(color, 1.0);
    }

    float s = smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, dist);
    return vec4(color, mix(1.0, 0.0, s));
}

GlassFragment glassRefraction(vec2 position, vec2 halfBlurSize, vec4 cornerRadius, float dist, float edgeFactor, float concaveFactor)
{
    const float h = 1.0;
    vec2 gradient = vec2(
            roundedRectangleDist(position + vec2(h, 0), halfBlurSize, cornerRadius) - roundedRectangleDist(position - vec2(h, 0), halfBlurSize, cornerRadius),
            roundedRectangleDist(position + vec2(0, h), halfBlurSize, cornerRadius) - roundedRectangleDist(position - vec2(0, h), halfBlurSize, cornerRadius)
    );

    vec2 normal = length(gradient) > 0.0 ? -normalize(gradient) : vec2(0.0, 1.0);

    float finalStrength = min(0.4 * concaveFactor * refractionStrength, 1.0);

    vec2 refractOffsetG = -normal.xy * finalStrength;
    vec2 refractOffsetR = -normal.xy * finalStrength;
    vec2 refractOffsetB = -normal.xy * finalStrength;

    // Different refraction offsets for each color channel
    float fringingFactor = refractionRGBFringing * 0.3;
    if (fringingFactor > 0.0) {
        // Red bends most
        refractOffsetR = -normal.xy * (finalStrength * (1.0 + fringingFactor));
        // Blue bends least
        refractOffsetB = -normal.xy * (finalStrength * (1.0 - fringingFactor));
    }

    vec2 coordR = clamp(uv - refractOffsetR, 0.0, 1.0);
    vec2 coordG = clamp(uv - refractOffsetG, 0.0, 1.0);
    vec2 coordB = clamp(uv - refractOffsetB, 0.0, 1.0);

    vec4 color = vec4(
        texture(texUnit, coordR).r,
        texture(texUnit, coordG).g,
        texture(texUnit, coordB).b,
        texture(texUnit, coordG).a
    );
    return GlassFragment(color, dist, edgeFactor, concaveFactor, vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0), 1.0);
}

### User

还是不行，我觉得问题出在qml上。不是kwin effect插件。我觉得qml写的有bug



### Assistant

你判断对了，而且我找到一个根本性的 QML bug：`RoundedBlurRegion` 把 `Region` 自带的 `item` 属性同时当作自定义目标项使用。这个属性会让根 Region 直接变成完整矩形，覆盖了后面用于圆角的几何绑定；所以“每个小块”的圆角区域实际上并不精确。

我会改成独立的 `targetItem` 属性，并更新所有调用点。这样镂空处不会被卡片的矩形 region 吞掉，圆角区域才是真正的圆角。

```

text(r.output);
)
```

### Assistant

对，就是这个原因。`BackgroundEffect` 是附着在一个 Wayland 窗口上的；控制中心虽在视觉上有八张卡，但对 KWin 来说仍是一张 `PopupWindow`，只拿到多个区域的并集。液态 shader 无法从这个并集可靠知道“这里是第几张卡、各自的圆角和光向”，因此会按外接区域计算，造成后方模糊和边界错位。[Quickshell 的 BackgroundEffect 文档](https://quickshell.org/docs/v0.3.0/types/Quickshell.Wayland/BackgroundEffect/)也明确它是作用于整个窗口 surface 的。

所以不是做不到，但不能再靠当前这层 QML `Region` 叠补。正确路线只有两条：

1. 重构控制中心为多张独立 Wayland 弹窗，每张小卡一张 surface——效果会像 Dock 一样精确，镂空完全不受影响。
2. 深改 KWin 插件，让它识别同一 surface 内的多个独立“玻璃岛”并逐块渲染——更底层、更复杂，但不用拆控制中心布局。

我已撤回刚才无效的 QML mask 实验。我的建议是走方案 1：视觉最确定、不会再出现整块外接框污染。你确认后我开始重构控制中心。

### User

现在开始你的优化吧：重构控制中心为多张独立 Wayland 弹窗，每张小卡一张 surface——效果会像 Dock 一样精确，镂空完全不受影响。
深改 KWin 插件，让它识别同一 surface 内的多个独立“玻璃岛”并逐块渲染——更底层、更复杂，但不用拆控制中心布局。 就是这两个方案。你觉得哪个比较合适？



### Assistant

我建议选方案 2：深改 KWin 插件的“多玻璃岛”渲染。

拆成多张 Wayland 弹窗看似直接，但会带来焦点、鼠标命中、弹出层层级、开关动画和窗口定位的连锁问题；八张独立 surface 也容易有合成接缝。它更适合真正互不相关的小部件，不适合一个整体控制中心。

KWin 多岛方案能保留现有控制中心布局和交互，正确做法是：

- 从 region 并集中识别彼此断开的卡片区域
- 每个区域独立计算外接框、圆角、UV、折射和高光
- 每块单独绘制，不再以控制中心整体外接框计算
- 间隙不生成任何顶点，因此完全保持原始壁纸

这比拆窗复杂，但正好从根上解决当前问题，而且效果会与 Dock 的单卡逻辑一致。我会按这个方向做，先把渲染管线改成支持多个独立 rounded island，再恢复控制中心的 compositor blur。

vec4 processSample(sampler2D tex, vec2 baseUv, vec3 glassNormal, float ior, float dispersion, float magnitude, vec2 uvScale, vec2 lensShift)
{
    vec3 viewRay = vec3(0.0, 0.0, -1.0);

    vec3 refractG = refract(viewRay, glassNormal, 1.0 / ior);
    vec2 dir = length(refractG.xy) > 0.001 ? normalize(refractG.xy) : vec2(0.0);
    vec2 shiftG = dir * magnitude * uvScale + lensShift;
    vec4 sampleG = texture(tex, clamp(baseUv + shiftG, 0.0, 1.0));

    if (dispersion > 0.001) {
        float fringe = clamp(dispersion, 0.0, 1.0) * 0.3;
        vec2 shiftR = dir * (magnitude * (1.0 + fringe)) * uvScale + lensShift;
        vec2 shiftB = dir * (magnitude * (1.0 - fringe)) * uvScale + lensShift;

        float r = texture(tex, clamp(baseUv + shiftR, 0.0, 1.0)).r;
        float b = texture(tex, clamp(baseUv + shiftB, 0.0, 1.0)).b;
        return vec4(r, sampleG.g, b, sampleG.a);
    }
    return sampleG;
}

现有渲染虽然把 region 拆成许多小矩形提交给显卡，但 shader 只有一套全局的 `box / blurSize / cornerRadius`。因此所有小矩形仍按“控制中心整体”计算液态折射和高光，才会产生错位蒙层。

A one-pixel join accounts for Region's band splitting.\n+static QList<Region> splitDisconnectedRegions(const Region &shape)\n+{\n+    QList<Region> islands;\n+    for (const Rect &rect : shape.rects()) {\n+        Region candidate(rect);\n+        for (qsizetype i = 0; i < islands.size();) {\n+            if (islands.at(i).grownBy(QMargins(1, 1, 1, 1)).intersects(candidate)) {\n+                candidate |= islands.takeAt(i);\n+                i = 0;\n+            } else {\n+                ++i;\n+            }\n+        }\n+        islands.append(std::move(candidate));\n+    }\n+    return islands;\n+}\n+#endif\n*** End Patch";
